<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ سَأَلَ ابْنَ شِهَابٍ عَنْ الاِسْتِثْنَاءِ، فِي الْحَجِّ فَقَالَ أَوَيَصْنَعُ ذَلِكَ أَحَدٌ وَأَنْكَرَ ذَلِكَ ‏.‏ سُئِلَ مَالِكٌ هَلْ يَحْتَشُّ الرَّجُلُ لِدَابَّتِهِ مِنَ الْحَرَمِ فَقَالَ لاَ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/956" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Ibn Shihab (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he asked Ibn Shihab about making a condition in the hajj that one could leave ihram at any place where an obstacle befell one and he said, &quot;Does anyone do that?&#x27; and disapproved of it.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 956]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/956" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik was asked whether a man could cut plants from the Haram for his mount, and he said, &quot;No</p>

## Narration 952

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A957)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 20</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 957]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/957" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 957</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   957 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ عُرْوَةَ بْنِ الزُّبَيْرِ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ أُمِّ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ، أَنَّهَا كَانَتْ تَقُولُ الصِّيَامُ لِمَنْ تَمَتَّعَ بِالْعُمْرَةِ إِلَى الْحَجِّ لِمَنْ لَمْ يَجِدْ هَدْيًا مَا بَيْنَ أَنْ يُهِلَّ بِالْحَجِّ إِلَى يَوْمِ عَرَفَةَ فَإِنْ لَمْ يَصُمْ صَامَ أَيَّامَ مِنًى ‏.‏ وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ سَالِمِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّهُ كَانَ يَقُولُ فِي ذَلِكَ مِثْلَ قَوْلِ عَائِشَةَ رضى الله تعالى عنها ‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/957" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">This source entry preserves two separately attributed reports in their original order. They remain one provider entry, with their distinct narrator introductions and source references. Report type: companion report. Attribution: A&#x27;isha (companion). Aisha’s supplied saying and Ibn Umar’s separately attributed abbreviated parallel remain distinct. The compiler’s opening rule remains context. Report type: abbreviated transmission. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Umar (companion). Aisha’s supplied saying and Ibn Umar’s separately attributed abbreviated parallel remain distinct. The compiler’s opening rule remains context.</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 1</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab from Urwa ibn az- Zubayr that A&#x27;isha umm al-muminin used to say, &quot;Someone performing hajj at-tamattu who does not have a sacrificial animal fasts (three days) from the time he enters ihram for the hajj till the Day of Arafa, and if he does not fast then, he fasts the days of Mina.&quot;</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 2</p>

<p dir="ltr"> Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab from Salim ibn Abdullah that Abdullah ibn Umar used to say the same concerning that, as the words of A&#x27;isha, may Allah the Exalted be pleased with her</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 957]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/957" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 957</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   957 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Malik said, concerning a woman who had never been on hajj, &quot;If she doesn&#x27;t have a mahram, or if she has, but he cannot come with her, she does not abandon Allah&#x27;s making of the hajj obligatory for her. Let her go in a group of women.&quot; </p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/957" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">उम्मुल मोमिनीन हज़रत आयशा (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हा) से रिवायत है; वो कहती थीं रोज़ा उस शख़्स के ऊपर है जो तमत्तुअ करे यानी उमराह करके हज्ज करे और हदी न पाए, हज्ज के एहराम से ले कर अरफ़ा तक रोज़े रखे, अगर इन दिनों में न रखे तो मिना के दिनों में रखे। अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) भी इस मुक़दमे में मिस्ल क़ौल-ए-आयशा के कहते थे।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/957" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">ام المومنین حضرت عائشہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہا سے روایت ہے ؛ وہ کہتی تھیں روزہ اس شخص کے اوپر ہے جو تمتع کرے یعنی عمرہ کر کے حج کرے اور ہدی نہ پائے حج کے احرام سے لے کر عرفہ تک روزے رکھے اگر ان دنوں میں نہ رکھے تو منی کے دنوں میں رکھے ۔ عبداللہ بن عمر بھی اس مقدمے میں مثل قول عائشہ کے کہتے ۔</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Umm-ul-Momineen Hazrat Aisha (Radi Allahu Anha) se riwayat hai; woh kehti thin roza us shakhs ke upar hai jo tamattu kare yani Umrah karke hajj kare aur hadi na paye, hajj ke ehram se le kar Arafah tak roze rakhe, agar in dino mein na rakhe toh Mina ke dino mein rakhe. Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) bhi is muqadme mein misl qaul-e-Aisha ke kehte the.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/957" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 953

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A959)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 21</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ أَبِي الزِّنَادِ، عَنِ الأَعْرَجِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ مَثَلُ الْمُجَاهِدِ فِي سَبِيلِ اللَّهِ كَمَثَلِ الصَّائِمِ الْقَائِمِ الدَّائِمِ الَّذِي لا يَفْتُرُ مِنْ صَلاَةٍ وَلاَ صِيَامٍ حَتَّى يَرْجِعَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 959]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/959" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 959</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   959 ]</p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abu&#x27;z-Zinad from al-Araj from Abu Hurayra that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said &quot;Someone who does jihad in the way of Allah is like someone who fasts and prays constantly and who does not slacken from his prayer and fasting until he returns</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 959]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/959" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू हुरैरह (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: जो शख्स अल्लाह की राह में जिहाद करे उसकी मिसाल ऐसी है जैसे कोई दिन भर रोजा रखे और रात भर इबादत करे और न रोज़े से यहाँ तक के लौटे जिहाद से.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 959]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/959" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابوہریرہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا جو شخص اللہ کی راہ میں جہاد کرے اس کی مثال ایسی ہے جیسے کوئی دن بھر روزہ رکھے اور رات بھر عبادت کرے، نہ تھکے نماز سے اور نہ روزے سے یہاں تک کہ لوٹے جہاد سے</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 959]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/959" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 954

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A960)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 21</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ أَبِي الزِّنَادِ، عَنِ الأَعْرَجِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ تَكَفَّلَ اللَّهُ لِمَنْ جَاهَدَ فِي سَبِيلِهِ - لاَ يُخْرِجُهُ مِنْ بَيْتِهِ إِلاَّ الْجِهَادُ فِي سَبِيلِهِ وَتَصْدِيقُ كَلِمَاتِهِ - أَنْ يُدْخِلَهُ الْجَنَّةَ أَوْ يَرُدَّهُ إِلَى مَسْكَنِهِ الَّذِي خَرَجَ مِنْهُ مَعَ مَا نَالَ مِنْ أَجْرٍ أَوْ غَنِيمَةٍ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 960]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/960" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 960</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   960 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abu&#x27;z Zinad from al-Araj from Abu Hurayra that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Allah guarantees either the Garden or a safe return to his home with whatever he has obtained of reward or booty, for the one who does jihad in His way, if it is solely jihad and trust in his promise that brings him out of his house</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 960]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/960" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू हुरैरा (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: अल्लाह ज़ामिन है उस शख़्स का जो जिहाद करे उसकी राह में, और न निकले घर से मगर जिहाद की नियत से, अल्लाह के कलाम को सच्चा जान कर; इस बात का के दाख़िल करेगा अल्लाह उसको जन्नत में या फेर लायेगा उसको उसके घर में जहाँ से निकला है सवाब और ग़नीमत के साथ।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 960]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/960" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابوہریرہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا اللہ ضامن ہے اس شخص کا جو جہاد کرے اس کی راہ میں، اور نہ نکلے گھر سے مگر جہاد کی نیت سے، اللہ کے کلام کو سچا جان کر؛ اس بات کا کہ داخل کرے گا اللہ اس کو جنت میں یا پھیر لائے گا اس کو اس کے گھر میں جہاں سے نکلا ہے ثواب اور غنیمت کے ساتھ ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 960]</p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Hurairah (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Allah zamin hai us shakhs ka jo jihad kare uski raah mein, aur na nikle ghar se magar jihad ki niyat se, Allah ke kalam ko sachcha jaan kar; is baat ka ke dakhil karega Allah usko Jannat mein ya pher layega usko uske ghar mein jahan se nikla hai sawab aur ghaneemat ke saath.</p>

## Narration 955

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A961)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 21</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ زَيْدِ بْنِ أَسْلَمَ، عَنْ أَبِي صَالِحٍ السَّمَّانِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏&quot;‏ الْخَيْلُ لِرَجُلٍ أَجْرٌ وَلِرَجُلٍ سِتْرٌ وَعَلَى رَجُلٍ وِزْرٌ فَأَمَّا الَّذِي هِيَ لَهُ أَجْرٌ فَرَجُلٌ رَبَطَهَا فِي سَبِيلِ اللَّهِ فَأَطَالَ لَهَا فِي مَرْجٍ أَوْ رَوْضَةٍ فَمَا أَصَابَتْ فِي طِيَلِهَا ذَلِكَ مِنَ الْمَرْجِ أَوِ الرَّوْضَةِ كَانَ لَهُ حَسَنَاتٌ وَلَوْ أَنَّهَا قَطَعَتْ طِيَلَهَا ذَلِكَ فَاسْتَنَّتْ شَرَفًا أَوْ شَرَفَيْنِ كَانَتْ آثَارُهَا وَأَرْوَاثُهَا حَسَنَاتٍ لَهُ وَلَوْ أَنَّهَا مَرَّتْ بِنَهَرٍ فَشَرِبَتْ مِنْهُ وَلَمْ يُرِدْ أَنْ يَسْقِيَ بِهِ كَانَ ذَلِكَ لَهُ حَسَنَاتٍ فَهِيَ لَهُ أَجْرٌ وَرَجُلٌ رَبَطَهَا تَغَنِّيًا وَتَعَفُّفًا وَلَمْ يَنْسَ حَقَّ اللَّهِ فِي رِقَابِهَا وَلاَ فِي ظُهُورِهَا فَهِيَ لِذَلِكَ سِتْرٌ وَرَجُلٌ رَبَطَهَا فَخْرًا وَرِيَاءً وَنِوَاءً لأَهْلِ الإِسْلاَمِ فَهِيَ عَلَى ذَلِكَ وِزْرٌ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ وَسُئِلَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم عَنِ الْحُمُرِ فَقَالَ ‏&quot;‏ لَمْ يَنْزِلْ عَلَىَّ فِيهَا شَىْءٌ إِلاَّ هَذِهِ الآيَةُ الْجَامِعَةُ الْفَاذَّةُ ‏{‏فَمَنْ يَعْمَلْ مِثْقَالَ ذَرَّةٍ خَيْرًا يَرَهُ وَمَنْ يَعْمَلْ مِثْقَالَ ذَرَّةٍ شَرًّا يَرَهُ ‏}‏‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 961]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/961" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Zayd ibn Aslam from Abu Salih as-Samman from Abu Hurayra that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Horses are a reward for one man, a protection for another, a burden for another. The one who has them as a reward is the one who dedicates them for use in the way of Allah, and tethers them in a meadow or grassland. Whatever the horse enjoys of the grassland or meadow in the length of its tether are good deeds for him. If it breaks its tether and goes over a hillock or two, its tracks and droppings are good deeds for him. If it crosses a river and drinks from it while he did not mean to allow it to drink it, that counts as good deeds for him, and the horse is a reward for him. Another man uses his horse to gain self reliance and up- standingness and does not forget Allah&#x27;s right on their necks and backs (i.e. he does not ill treat or over-work them). Horses are a protection for him . Another man uses them out of pride to show them off and in hostility to the people of Islam. They are a burden on that man.&quot; The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, was asked about donkeys, and he said, &quot;Nothing has been revealed to me about them except this single all- inclusive ayat, &#x27;Whoever does an atom of good will see it, and whoever does an atom of evil, will see it.&#x27; &quot; (Sura 99 Ayats)</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 961]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/961" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू हुरैरा (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: घोड़े एक शख़्स के वास्ते अजर हैं, एक शख़्स के वास्ते दुरुस्त हैं और एक शख़्स के वास्ते गुनाह हैं। अजर उसके वास्ते हैं जो इन्हें जिहाद के लिए बांधे, फिर उनकी रस्सी लंबी कर दे किसी चरागाह या मैदान में, तो जितनी दूर तक वो रस्सी के सबब चरेंगे उसके वास्ते नेकियां लिखी जायेंगी। अगर वो रस्सी तोड़ कर किसी ऊँचाई या नीचाई पर चढ़ें तो उनके हर क़दम और लीद पर नेकियां लिखी जायेंगी, और अगर वो किसी नहर पर जा निकलें और पानी पी लें जबकि मालिक का इरादा पानी पिलाने का न था, तब भी उसके वास्ते नेकियां लिखी जायेंगी। दुरुस्त उसके वास्ते हैं जो तिजारत के लिए बांधे और उनकी ज़कात अदा करे। और गुनाह उसके वास्ते हैं जो फ़ख़्र, रिया और मुसलमानों की दुश्मनी के लिए बांधे। आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से गधों के बारे में सवाल हुआ तो फ़रमाया: इस मुआमले में मुझ पर कुछ नहीं उतरा सिवाए इस आयत के जो अकेली तमाम नेकियों को शामिल है: &#x27;फ़मन यअमल मिस्काला ज़र्रतिन ख़यरन यरह, व मन यअमल मिस्काला ज़र्रतिन शर्रन यरह&#x27;।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 961]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/961" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابوہریرہ رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا گھوڑے ایک شخص کے واسطے اجر ہیں اور ایک شخص کے واسطے درست ہیں اور ایک شخص کے واسطے گناہ ہیں؛ اجر اس کے واسطے ہیں جو باندھے ان کو جہاد کے واسطے پھر لمبی کردے اس کی رسی ، ان کی کسی موضع یا چراگاہ میں تو جس قدر دور تک اس رسی کے سبب سے چرے اس کی واسطے نیکیاں لکھی جائیں گی ، اگر وہ رسی توڑا کر ایک اونچان یا نیچان چڑھیں ان کے ہر قدم اور لید پر نیکیاں لکھی جائیں گی اور اگر وہ کسی نہر پر جا نکلے اور پانی پائے اور مالک کا ارادہ پانی پلانے کا نہ تھا تب بھی اس کے واسطے نیکیاں لکھی جائیں۔ اور درست اس کے واسطے ہیں جو تجارت کے واسطے باندھے اور ان کی زکوة ادا کرے۔ اور گناہ اس کے واسطے ہیں جو فخر اور ریا اور مسلمانوں کی دشمنی کے لئے باندھے۔ اور حضرت سے سوال ہوا گدھوں کے باب میں، آپ نے فرمایا کہ اس مقدمے میں میرے اوپر کچھ نہیں اترا مگر یہ آیت جو اکیلی تمام نیکیوں کو شامل ہے &quot;فَمَنْ يَّعْمَلْ مِثْقَالَ ذَرَّةٍ خَيْرًا يَّرَه Ċ وَمَنْ يَّعْمَلْ مِثْقَالَ ذَرَّةٍ شَرًّا يَّرَه Ď &quot; 99۔ الزلہ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 961]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/961" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Hurairah (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Ghode ek shakhs ke vaste ajar hain, ek shakhs ke vaste durust hain aur ek shakhs ke vaste gunah hain. Ajar uske vaste hain jo inhe Jihad ke liye bandhe, phir unki rassi lambi karde kisi charagah ya maidan mein, to jitni door tak woh rassi ke sabab charenge uske vaste nekiyan likhi jayengi. Agar woh rassi tod kar kisi unchai ya nichai par chadein to unke har qadam aur leed par nekiyan likhi jayengi, aur agar woh kisi nehar par ja nikle aur pani pi lein jabke malik ka irada pani pilane ka na tha, tab bhi uske vaste nekiyan likhi jayengi. Durust uske vaste hain jo tijarat ke liye bandhe aur unki Zakat ada kare. Aur gunah uske vaste hain jo fakhr, riya aur musalmanon ki dushmani ke liye bandhe. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se gadho ke bare mein sawal hua to farmaya: Is muamle mein mujh par kuch nahi utra siwaye is ayat ke jo akeli tamam nekiyon ko shamil hai: &#x27;Faman ya&#x27;mal mithqala dharratin khayran yarah, wa man ya&#x27;mal mithqala dharratin sharran yarah&#x27;.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 961]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/961" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 956

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A962)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 21</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ مَعْمَرٍ الأَنْصَارِيِّ، عَنْ عَطَاءِ بْنِ يَسَارٍ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ أَلاَ أُخْبِرُكُمْ بِخَيْرِ النَّاسِ مَنْزِلاً رَجُلٌ آخِذٌ بِعِنَانِ فَرَسِهِ يُجَاهِدُ فِي سَبِيلِ اللَّهِ أَلاَ أُخْبِرُكُمْ بِخَيْرِ النَّاسِ مَنْزِلاً بَعْدَهُ رَجُلٌ مُعْتَزِلٌ فِي غُنَيْمَتِهِ يُقِيمُ الصَّلاَةَ وَيُؤْتِي الزَّكَاةَ وَيَعْبُدُ اللَّهَ لاَ يُشْرِكُ بِهِ شَيْئًا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 962]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/962" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Abdullah ibn Abd ar-Rahman ibn Mamar al- Ansari that Ata ibn Yasar said that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Shall I tell you who has the best degree among people? A man who takes the rein of his horse to do jihad in the way of Allah. Shall I tell you who has the best degree among people after him? A man who lives alone with a few sheep, performs the prayer, pays the zakat, and worships Allah without associating anything with him</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 962]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/962" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अत बिन यसर से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: क्या मैं न बताऊँ तुमको वह शख्स जो सबसे बढ़कर दर्जा रखता है? वह शख्स है जो अपने घोड़े की लगाम पकड़े हुए जिहाद करता है अल्लाह की राह में. क्या मैं न बताऊँ तुमको उसके बाद जो सबसे बढ़कर दर्जा रखता है? वह शख्स है जो एक गोशे में बकरियों का गल्ला लेकर नमाज़ पढता है और अल्लाह को पूजता है</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 962]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/962" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عطاء بن یسار سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: کیا میں نہ بتاؤں تم کو وہ شخص جو سب سے بڑھ کر درجہ رکھتا ہے؛ وہ شخص ہے جو اپنے گھوڑے کی لگام پکڑے ہوئے جہاد کرتا ہے اللہ کی راہ میں، کیا نہ بتاؤں میں تم کو اس کے بعد جو سب سے بڑھ کر درجہ رکھتا ہے وہ شخص ہے جو ایک گوشے میں بکریوں کا غلہ لے کر نماز پڑھتا ہے اور اللہ کو پوجتا ہے، اس کے ساتھ کسی کو شریک نہیں کرتا۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 962]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/962" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Ata bin Yasar se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Kya main na bataun tumko woh shakhs jo sabse badhkar darja rakhta hai? Woh shakhs hai jo apne ghode ki lagaam pakde hue Jihad karta hai Allah ki rah mein. Kya main na bataun tumko uske baad jo sabse badhkar darja rakhta hai? Woh shakhs hai jo ek goshe mein bakriyon ka galla lekar namaz padhta hai aur Allah ko poojta hai, uske saath kisi ko shareek nahi karta.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 962]</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، قَالَ أَخْبَرَنِي عُبَادَةُ بْنُ الْوَلِيدِ بْنِ عُبَادَةَ بْنِ الصَّامِتِ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ جَدِّهِ، قَالَ بَايَعْنَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم عَلَى السَّمْعِ وَالطَّاعَةِ فِي الْيُسْرِ وَالْعُسْرِ وَالْمَنْشَطِ وَالْمَكْرَهِ وَأَنْ لاَ نُنَازِعَ الأَمْرَ أَهْلَهُ وَأَنْ نَقُولَ أَوْ نَقُومَ بِالْحَقِّ حَيْثُمَا كُنَّا لاَ نَخَافُ فِي اللَّهِ لَوْمَةَ لاَئِمٍ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/963" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Ubada ibn as-Samit describing the pledge made with the Messenger of Allah. The entire field supplies the pledge conditions in ease and difficulty, authority and speaking truth.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that Yahya ibn Said said, &#x27;&#x27;Ubada ibn al-Walid ibn Ubada ibn as-Samit informed me from his father that his grandfather (Ubada) said, &#x27;We made a contract with the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, to hear and obey in ease and hardship, enthusiasm and reluctance, and not to dispute with people in authority and to speak or establish the truth wherever we were without worrying about criticism</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 963]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/963" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">उबादह बिन सामित (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के हमने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से बैत की सुनने और इताअत करने पर, आसानी और सख्ती में, खुशी और गम में। हमने इस बात पर बैत की के जो मुसलमान हुकूमत के लायक होगा उससे न झगड़ेंगे और इस अमर पर के हम सच कहेंगे या सच पर जमे रहेंगे जहां भी होंगे, और अल्लाह के काम में किसी के बुरा कहने से न डरेंगे।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/963" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="rtl">عبادہ بن صامت رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ سے روایت ہے کہ ہم نے بیعت کی رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے سننے اور اطاعت کرنے پر آسانی اور سختی میں خوشی اور غم میں، اور بیعت کی ہم نے اس بات پر کہ جو مسلماں حکومت کے لائق ہوگا اس سے نہ جھگڑیں گے اور اس امر پر کہ ہم سچ کہیں گے یا سچ پر جمے رہیں گے جہاں ہوں گے، اللہ کے کام میں کسی کے برا کہنے سے نہ ڈریں گے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ زَيْدِ بْنِ أَسْلَمَ، قَالَ كَتَبَ أَبُو عُبَيْدَةَ بْنُ الْجَرَّاحِ إِلَى عُمَرَ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ يَذْكُرُ لَهُ جُمُوعًا مِنَ الرُّومِ وَمَا يَتَخَوَّفُ مِنْهُمْ فَكَتَبَ إِلَيْهِ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ أَمَّا بَعْدُ فَإِنَّهُ مَهْمَا يَنْزِلْ بِعَبْدٍ مُؤْمِنٍ مِنْ مُنْزَلِ شِدَّةٍ يَجْعَلِ اللَّهُ بَعْدَهُ فَرَجًا وَإِنَّهُ لَنْ يَغْلِبَ عُسْرٌ يُسْرَيْنِ وَأَنَّ اللَّهَ تَعَالَى يَقُولُ فِي كِتَابِهِ ‏{‏يَا أَيُّهَا الَّذِينَ آمَنُوا اصْبِرُوا وَصَابِرُوا وَرَابِطُوا وَاتَّقُوا اللَّهَ لَعَلَّكُمْ تُفْلِحُونَ ‏}‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/964" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that Zayd ibn Aslam had said that Ubayda ibn al-Jarrah had written to Umar ibn al-Khattab mentioning to him a great array of Byzantine troops and the anxiety they were causing him. Umar ibn al-Khattab wrote in reply to him, &quot;Whatever hardship befalls a believing slave, Allah will make an opening for him after it, and a hardship will not overcome two eases. Allah the Exalted says in His Book, &#x27;O you who trust, be patient, and vie in patience; be steadfast and fear Allah, perhaps you will profit.&#x27; &quot; (Sura 3 ayat)</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 964]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/964" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">ज़ैद बिन असलम से रिवायत है के अबू उबैदाह बिन जर्राह ने हज़रत उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) को रूम के लश्कर और अपने खौफ का हाल लिखा। हज़रत उमर (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने जवाब लिखा: बाद हम्द-ओ-नाअत के मालूम हो के बंदा-ए-मोमिन पर जब कोई सख्ती उतरती है तो उसके बाद अल्लाह पाक खुशी देता है, और एक सख्ती दो आसानियों पर ग़ालिब नहीं हो सकती। और बेशक अल्लाह तआला ने अपनी किताब में फरमाया: &#x27;ऐ ईमान वालो! सब्र करो मुसीबतों पर, और सब्र करो कुफ्फार के मुकाबले में, और कायम रहो जिहाद पर और डरो अल्लाह से शायद के तुम निजात पाओ&#x27;।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/964" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">زید بن اسلم سے روایت ہے کہ ابوعبیدہ بن جراح نے حضرت عمر کو روم کے لشکروں کا اور اپنے خوف کا حال لکھا ؛ حضرت عمر نے جواب لکھا کہ بعد حمد ونعت کے معلوم ہو کہ بندہ مومن پر جب کوئی سختی اترتی ہے تو اس کے بعد اللہ پاک خوشی دیتا ہے؛ اور ایک سختی دو آسانیوں پر غالب نہیں ہو سکتی؛ اور بے شک اللہ تعالیٰ نے اپنی کتاب میں فرمایا ؛ &quot; اے ایمان والو صبر کرو مصیبتوں پر اور صبر کرو کفار کے مقابلہ میں اور قائم رہو جہاد پر اور ڈرو اللہ سے شاید کہ تم نجات پاؤ&quot; ۔</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Zaid bin Aslam se riwayat hai ke Abu Ubaidah bin Jarrah ne Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ko Room ke lashkar aur apne khauf ka haal likha. Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne jawab likha: Baad hamd-o-naat ke maloom ho ke banda-e-momin par jab koi sakhti utarti hai to uske baad Allah Pak khushi deta hai, aur ek sakhti do aasaniyon par ghalib nahi ho sakti. Aur beshak Allah Ta&#x27;ala ne apni kitab mein farmaya: &#x27;Aye imaan walo! Sabr karo museebaton par, aur sabr karo kuffar ke muqable mein, aur qaim raho Jihad par aur daro Allah se shayad ke tum nijat pao&#x27;.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/964" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ نَهَى رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَنْ يُسَافَرَ بِالْقُرْآنِ إِلَى أَرْضِ الْعَدُوِّ ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: The Messenger of Allah in Abdullah ibn Umars report. Select the actual prohibition concerning travelling with a Quran into enemy land. Maliks subsequent explanation of its rationale remains context.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi that Abdullah ibn Umar said that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, forbade travelling with a Qur&#x27;an in the land of the enemy. </p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 965]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/965" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنِ ابْنٍ لِكَعْبِ بْنِ مَالِكٍ، - قَالَ حَسِبْتُ أَنَّهُ قَالَ عَنْ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ كَعْبٍ، أَنَّهُ - قَالَ نَهَى رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم الَّذِينَ قَتَلُوا ابْنَ أَبِي الْحُقَيْقِ عَنْ قَتْلِ النِّسَاءِ وَالْوِلْدَانِ - قَالَ - فَكَانَ رَجُلٌ مِنْهُمْ يَقُولُ بَرَّحَتْ بِنَا امْرَأَةُ ابْنِ أَبِي الْحُقَيْقِ بِالصِّيَاحِ فَأَرْفَعُ السَّيْفَ عَلَيْهَا ثُمَّ أَذْكُرُ نَهْىَ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَأَكُفُّ وَلَوْلاَ ذَلِكَ اسْتَرَحْنَا مِنْهَا ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/966" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">This reading preserves separate source spans. Intervening source notes are retained under Comments. Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: The Messenger of Allah prohibiting killing women and children, followed by a participants supplied recollection. The whole field supplies the prohibition and a fighters reported restraint because of it. Maliks uncertain identification of the intermediary and the editorial description of Ibn Abi Huqayq remain context. Some reports contain separate source passages. Intervening source text is retained under Comments.</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 1</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab that a son of Kab ibn Malik</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Intervening source text is retained under Comments.</p>

<p dir="ltr"> said, &quot;The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, forbade those who fought ibn Abi Huqayq</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Intervening source text is retained under Comments.</p>

<p dir="ltr"> to kill women and children. He said that one of the men fighting had said, &#x27;The wife of ibn Abi Huqayq began screaming and I repeatedly raised my sword against her. Then I would remember the prohibition of the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, so I would stop. Had it not been for that, we would have been rid of her</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 966]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/966" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुर्रहमान बिन काअब से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने उन लोगों को मना किया था जिन्होंने इब्न अबी हकिक को क़त्ल किया था, के वह औरतों और बच्चों को क़त्ल न करें। इब्न काअब ने कहा: हम में से एक शख़्स कहता था के इब्न अबी हकिक की औरत ने चीख़ कर हमारा हाल खोल दिया था, तो मैं तलवार उस पर उठाता था फिर रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) की मुमानत याद करके रुक जाता था; अगर ऐसा न होता तो हम उसे भी क़त्ल कर देते।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/966" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">عبدالرحمن بن کعب سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے منع کیا تھا ان لوگوں کو جہنوں نے قتل کیا ابن ابی حقیق کو عورتوں اور بچوں کے قتل کرنے سے ; ابن کعب نے کہا کہ ایک شخص ان میں سے کہتا تھا کہ ابن ابی حقیق کی عورت نے چیخ کر ہمارا حال کھول دیا تھا; تو میں تلوار اس پر اٹھاتا تھا پھر رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کی ممانعت کو یاد کر کے رک جاتا تھا، اگر ایسا نہ ہوتا تو ہم اسے بھی قتل کردیتے ۔</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Abdurrahman bin Ka&#x27;ab se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne un logon ko mana kiya tha jinhone Ibn Abi Haqiq ko qatl kiya tha, ke woh aurton aur bachon ko qatl na karein. Ibn Ka&#x27;ab ne kaha: Hum mein se ek shakhs kehta tha ke Ibn Abi Haqiq ki aurat ne cheekh kar hamara haal khol diya tha, to main talwar us par uthata tha phir Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ki mumanat yaad karke ruk jata tha; agar aisa na hota to hum use bhi qatl kar dete.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/966" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

## Narration 961

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A967)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 21</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم رَأَى فِي بَعْضِ مَغَازِيهِ امْرَأَةً مَقْتُولَةً فَأَنْكَرَ ذَلِكَ وَنَهَى عَنْ قَتْلِ النِّسَاءِ وَالصِّبْيَانِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 967]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/967" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 967</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   967 ]</p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi from Ibn Umar that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, saw the corpse of a woman who had been slain in one of the raids, and he disapproved of it and forbade the killing of women and children</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 967]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/967" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">नाफे&#x27; से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने बाज़ लड़ाइयों में एक औरत को क़त्ल किया हुआ पाया तो उसके क़त्ल को नापसंद किया, और औरतों और बच्चों के क़त्ल से मना फरमाया।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 967]</p>

## Narration 962

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A968)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 21</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 968]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/968" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 968</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   968 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، أَنَّ أَبَا بَكْرٍ الصِّدِّيقَ، بَعَثَ جُيُوشًا إِلَى الشَّامِ فَخَرَجَ يَمْشِي مَعَ يَزِيدَ بْنِ أَبِي سُفْيَانَ - وَكَانَ أَمِيرَ رُبْعٍ مِنْ تِلْكَ الأَرْبَاعِ - فَزَعَمُوا أَنَّ يَزِيدَ قَالَ لأَبِي بَكْرٍ إِمَّا أَنْ تَرْكَبَ وَإِمَّا أَنْ أَنْزِلَ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ أَبُو بَكْرٍ مَا أَنْتَ بِنَازِلٍ وَمَا أَنَا بِرَاكِبٍ إِنِّي أَحْتَسِبُ خُطَاىَ هَذِهِ فِي سَبِيلِ اللَّهِ ثُمَّ قَالَ لَهُ إِنَّكَ سَتَجِدُ قَوْمًا زَعَمُوا أَنَّهُمْ حَبَّسُوا أَنْفُسَهُمْ لِلَّهِ فَذَرْهُمْ وَمَا زَعَمُوا أَنَّهُمْ حَبَّسُوا أَنْفُسَهُمْ لَهُ وَسَتَجِدُ قَوْمًا فَحَصُوا عَنْ أَوْسَاطِ رُءُوسِهِمْ مِنَ الشَّعَرِ فَاضْرِبْ مَا فَحَصُوا عَنْهُ بِالسَّيْفِ وَإِنِّي مُوصِيكَ بِعَشْرٍ لاَ تَقْتُلَنَّ امْرَأَةً وَلاَ صَبِيًّا وَلاَ كَبِيرًا هَرِمًا وَلاَ تَقْطَعَنَّ شَجَرًا مُثْمِرًا وَلاَ تُخَرِّبَنَّ عَامِرًا وَلاَ تَعْقِرَنَّ شَاةً وَلاَ بَعِيرًا إِلاَّ لِمَأْكُلَةٍ وَلاَ تَحْرِقَنَّ نَحْلاً وَلاَ تُفَرِّقَنَّهُ وَلاَ تَغْلُلْ وَلاَ تَجْبُنْ ‏.‏</p>

<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 968]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/968" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 968</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   968 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Yahya ibn Said that Abu Bakr as-Siddiq was sending armies to ash-Sham. He went for a walk with Yazid ibn Abi Sufyan who was the commander of one of the battalions. It is claimed that Yazid said to Abu Bakr, &quot;Will you ride or shall I get down?&quot; Abu Bakrsaid, &quot;I will not ride and you will not get down. I intend these steps of mine to be in the way of Allah.&quot; Then Abu Bakr advised Yazid, &quot;You will find a people who claim to have totally given themselves to Allah. Leave them to what they claim to have given themselves. You will find a people who have shaved the middle of their heads, strike what they have shaved with the sword. &quot;I advise you ten things:Do not kill women or children or an aged, infirm person. Do not cut down fruit-bearing trees. Do not destroy an inhabited place. Do not slaughter sheep or camels except for food. Do not burn bees and do not scatter them. Do not steal from the booty, and do not be cowardly</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/968" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 968]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/968" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 968</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   968 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">यह्या बिन सईद से रिवायत है के हज़रत अबू बक्र सिद्दीक (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने जब शाम को लश्कर भेजा तो यज़ीद बिन अबी सुफियान के साथ पैदल चले। यज़ीद ने कहा: आप सवार हो जाएं नहीं तो मैं उतरता हूं। अबू बक्र सिद्दीक (रदी अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा: न तुम उतरो और न मैं सवार होऊंगा, मैं इन कदमों को अल्लाह की राह में सवाब समझता हूं। फिर फरमाया: तुम कुछ लोग ऐसे पाओगे जो अपनी जानों को रोक रखे हैं अल्लाह के वास्ते, उन्हें उनके काम में छोड़ दो। और कुछ लोग ऐसे पाओगे जो सर मुंडाते हैं, तो उनके सर पर तलवार चलाओ। मैं तुम्हें दस बातों की नसीहत करता हूं: औरत को मत मारो, बच्चे और बुढ़े को मत मारो, फल-दार दरख़्त न काटो, कोई बस्ती उजाड़ो मत, बकरी या ऊंट की कूंर्चे न काटो सिवाए खाने के, खजूर के दरख़्त न जलाओ और न डूबाओ, ग़नीमत के माल में चोरी न करो और न नामर्दी दिखाओ। उमर बिन अब्दुल अज़ीज़ ने अपने आमिलों को लिखा के हमें रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) की ये रिवायत पहुंची है के जब फौज रवाना करते तो कहते: अल्लाह का नाम लेकर जिहाद करो, तुम उन लोगों से लड़ते हो जिन्होंने अल्लाह के साथ कुफ्र किया। चोरी न करो, इकरार न तोड़ो, नाक-कान न काटो, बच्चों और औरतों को न मारो, और ये अमर अपनी फौजों से कह दो, अगर अल्लाह ने चाहा तो तुम पर सलामती होगी।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/968" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">یحیی بن سیعد سے روایت ہے کہ حضرت ابوبکر صدیق نے شام کو لشکر بھیجا تو یزید بن ابی سفیان کے ساتھ پیدل چلے اور وہ حاکم تھے ایک چوتھائی لشکر کے؛ تو یزید نے ابوبکر سے کہا آپ سوار ہو جائیں نہیں تو میں اترتا ہوں، ابوبکر صدیق نے کہا نہ تم اترو اور نہ میں سوار ہوں گا ، میں ان قدموں کو اللہ کی راہ میں ثواب سمجھتا ہوں پھر کہا یزید سے کہ تم پاؤ گے کچھ لوگ ایسے جو سمجھتے ہیں کہ ہم نے اپنی جانوں کو روک رکھا ہے اللہ کے واسطے سو چھوڑ دے ان کو اپنے کام میں اور کچھ لوگ ایسے پاؤ گے جو بیچ میں سے سر منڈاتے ہیں تو مار ان کے سر پر تلوار سے اور میں تجھ کو دس باتوں کی وصیت کرتا ہوں عورت کو مت مار اور نہ بچوں کو نہ بڈھے پھونس کو اور نہ کاٹنا پھل دار درخت کو اور نہ اجاڑنا کسی بستی کو اور نہ کونچیں کاٹنا کسی بکری اور اونٹ کی مگر کھانے کے واسطے اور مت جلانا کھجور کے درخت کو اور مت ڈبانا اس کو اور غنیمت کے مال میں چوری نہ کرنا اور نامردی نہ کرنا ۔ امام مالک نے روایت نقل کی ہے کہ عمر بن عبدالعزیز نے اپنے عاملوں میں سے ایک عامل کو لکھا کہ </p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/968" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="rtl"> اور کہہ دے یہ امر اپنی فوجوں اور لشکروں سے، اگر اللہ نے چاہا تو تم پر سلامتی ہوگی۔</p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 968]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/968" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Roman Urdu · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 968</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   968 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya bin Saeed se riwayat hai ke Hazrat Abu Bakr Siddiq (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne jab Shaam ko lashkar bheja to Yazid bin Abi Sufyan ke sath paidal chale. Yazid ne kaha: Aap sawar ho jayen nahi to main utarta hoon. Abu Bakr Siddiq (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha: Na tum utro aur na main sawar hounga, main in qadmon ko Allah ki rah mein sawab samajhta hoon. Phir farmaya: Tum kuch log aise paoge jo apni janon ko rok rakhe hain Allah ke vaste, unhe unke kaam mein chod do. Aur kuch log aise paoge jo sar mundate hain, to unke sar par talwar chalao. Main tumhe das baton ki nasihat karta hoon: Aurat ko mat maro, bache aur budhe ko mat maro, phal-dar darakht na kaato, koi basti ujado mat, bakri ya oont ki kounchein na kaato siwaye khane ke, khajoor ke darakht na jalao aur na dubao, ghanimat ke maal mein chori na karo aur na namardi dikhao. Umar bin Abdul Aziz ne apne amilon ko likha ke humein Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ki ye riwayat pahunchi hai ke jab fauj rawana karte to kehte: Allah ka naam lekar Jihad karo, tum un logon se ladte ho jinhone Allah ke sath kufr kiya. Chori na karo, iqrar na todo, naak-kaan na kaato, bachon aur aurton ko na maro, aur ye amar apni faujon se keh do, agar Allah ne chaha to tum par salamti hogi.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/968" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 963

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A969)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 21</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ عُمَرَ بْنَ عَبْدِ الْعَزِيزِ، كَتَبَ إِلَى عَامِلٍ مِنْ عُمَّالِهِ أَنَّهُ بَلَغَنَا أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم كَانَ إِذَا بَعَثَ سَرِيَّةً يَقُولُ لَهُمُ ‏ &quot;‏ اغْزُوا بِاسْمِ اللَّهِ فِي سَبِيلِ اللَّهِ تُقَاتِلُونَ مَنْ كَفَرَ بِاللَّهِ لاَ تَغُلُّوا وَلاَ تَغْدِرُوا وَلاَ تُمَثِّلُوا وَلاَ تَقْتُلُوا وَلِيدًا وَقُلْ ذَلِكَ لِجِيُوشِكَ وَسَرَايَاكَ إِنْ شَاءَ اللَّهُ وَالسَّلاَمُ عَلَيْكَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 969]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/969" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that Umar ibn Abd al-Aziz wrote to one of his governors, &quot;It has been passed down to us that when the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, sent out a raiding party, he would say to them, &#x27;Make your raids in the name of Allah in the way of Allah. Fight whoever denies Allah. Do not steal from the booty, and do not act treacherously. Do not mutilate and do not kill children.&#x27; Say the same to your armies and raiding parties, Allah willing. Peace be upon you</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 969]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/969" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   969 ]</p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">यहया ने मुझ से इमाम मालिक की सनद से बयान किया के उन्होंने सुना के उमर इब्न अब्द अल-अज़ीज़ रहमतुल्लाह ने अपने एक गवर्नर को लिखा, &#x27;हम तक यह बात पहुंची है के जब अल्लाह के रसूल (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) किसी लश्कर को रवाना फरमाते तो उन से कहते, अल्लाह के नाम से और अल्लाह की राह में जिहाद करो। जो अल्लाह का इनकार करे उस से लड़ो। माल-ए-गनीमत में खयानत न करो, अहद-शिकनी न करो, मुसला न करो (अज़ा न काटो) और बच्चों को क़त्ल न करो।&#x27; अपने लश्करों को भी यही नसीहत करो, इंशा अल्लाह। तुम पर सलामती हो।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 969]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/969" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">یحییٰ نے مجھ سے امام مالک کی سند سے بیان کیا کہ انہوں نے سنا کہ عمر بن عبد العزیز رحمہ اللہ نے اپنے ایک گورنر کو لکھا، &#x27;ہم تک یہ بات پہنچی ہے کہ جب اللہ کے رسول صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کسی لشکر کو روانہ فرماتے تو ان سے کہتے، اللہ کے نام سے اور اللہ کی راہ میں جہاد کرو۔ جو اللہ کا انکار کرے اس سے لڑو۔ مال غنیمت میں خیانت نہ کرو، عہد شکنی نہ کرو، مثلہ نہ کرو (اعضا نہ کاٹو) اور بچوں کو قتل نہ کرو۔&#x27; اپنے لشکروں کو بھی یہی نصیحت کرو، ان شاء اللہ۔ تم پر سلامتی ہو۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 969]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/969" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya ne mujh se Imam Malik ki sanad se bayan kiya ke unhon ne suna ke Umar ibn Abd al-Aziz Rahmatullah ne apne ek governor ko likha, &#x27;Hum tak yeh baat pohanchi hai ke jab Allah ke Rasool (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) kisi lashkar ko rawana farmate to un se kehte, Allah ke naam se aur Allah ki rah mein jihad karo. Jo Allah ka inkar kare us se laro. Maal-e-ghanimat mein khayanat na karo, ahd-shikani na karo, musla na karo (aza na kato) aur bachon ko qatl na karo.&#x27; Apne lashkaron ko bhi yahi nasihat karo, Insha Allah. Tum par salamti ho.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 969]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/969" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 964

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A970)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 21</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 970]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/970" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 970</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   970 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ رَجُلٍ، مِنْ أَهْلِ الْكُوفَةِ أَنَّ عُمَرَ بْنَ الْخَطَّابِ، كَتَبَ إِلَى عَامِلِ جَيْشٍ كَانَ بَعَثَهُ إِنَّهُ بَلَغَنِي أَنَّ رِجَالاً مِنْكُمْ يَطْلُبُونَ الْعِلْجَ حَتَّى إِذَا أَسْنَدَ فِي الْجَبَلِ وَامْتَنَعَ قَالَ رَجُلٌ مَطْرَسْ - يَقُولَ لاَ تَخَفْ - فَإِذَا أَدْرَكَهُ قَتَلَهُ وَإِنِّي وَالَّذِي نَفْسِي بِيَدِهِ لاَ أَعْلَمُ مَكَانَ وَاحِدٍ فَعَلَ ذَلِكَ إِلاَّ ضَرَبْتُ عُنُقَهُ ‏.‏ قَالَ يَحْيَى سَمِعْتُ مَالِكًا يَقُولُ لَيْسَ هَذَا الْحَدِيثُ بِالْمُجْتَمَعِ عَلَيْهِ وَلَيْسَ عَلَيْهِ الْعَمَلُ ‏.‏ وَسُئِلَ مَالِكٌ عَنِ الإِشَارَةِ بِالأَمَانِ أَهِيَ بِمَنْزِلَةِ الْكَلاَمِ فَقَالَ نَعَمْ وَإِنِّي أَرَى أَنْ يُتَقَدَّمَ إِلَى الْجُيُوشِ أَنْ لاَ تَقْتُلُوا أَحَدًا أَشَارُوا إِلَيْهِ بِالأَمَانِ لأَنَّ الإِشَارَةَ عِنْدِي بِمَنْزِلَةِ الْكَلاَمِ وَإِنَّهُ بَلَغَنِي أَنَّ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عَبَّاسٍ قَالَ مَا خَتَرَ قَوْمٌ بِالْعَهْدِ إِلاَّ سَلَّطَ اللَّهُ عَلَيْهِمُ الْعَدُوَّ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/970" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">This source entry preserves two separately attributed reports in their original order. They remain one provider entry, with their distinct narrator introductions and source references. Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion). The two separately attributed reports remain distinct. Malik’s explicit criticism that the first tradition is not unanimously agreed upon and not acted on stays visible as source context, alongside his legal discussion; it is not suppressed or replaced with an invented authenticity verdict. Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Abbas (companion). The two separately attributed reports remain distinct. Malik’s explicit criticism that the first tradition is not unanimously agreed upon and not acted on stays visible as source context, alongside his legal discussion; it is not suppressed or replaced with an invented authenticity verdict.</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 1</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from a man of Kufa that Umar ibn al-Khattab wrote to a lieutenant of an army which he had sent out, &quot;I have heard that it is the habit of some of your men to chase an unbeliever till he takes refuge in a high place. Then one man tells him in Persian not to be afraid, and when he comes up to him, he kills him. By He in whose hand my self is, if I knew someone who had done that, I would strike off his head.&quot;</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 2</p>

<p dir="ltr">I have heard that Abdullah ibn Abbas said, &#x27;There is no people who betray a pledge, but that Allah gives their enemies power over them</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 970]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/970" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 970</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   970 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Yahya said, I heard Malik say, &quot;This tradition is not unanimously agreed upon, so one does not act on it.&quot; Malik when asked whether safe conduct promised by gesture had the same status as that promised by speech, said, &quot;Yes. I think that one can request an army not to kill someone by gesturing for safe conduct, because as far as I am concerned, gesture has the same status as speech. </p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/970" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   970 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">कूफ़ा के रहने वाले से रिवायत है के हज़रत उमर बिन खत्ताब (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने लश्कर के एक अफ़सर को लिखा: मुझे ये ख़बर पहुंची है के बाज़ लोग तुम में से काफ़िर अजमी को बुलाते हैं जब वो पहाड़ पर चढ़ जाता है और लड़ाई से बाज़ आता है, तो एक शख़्स उससे कहता है मत डर, फिर क़ाबू पाकर उसको मार डालता है। क़सम उस ज़ात की जिसके क़ब्ज़े में मेरी जान है, अगर मैं किसी को ऐसा करते जान लूँगा तो उसकी गर्दन मार दूँगा।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/970" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">ایک کوفہ کے رہنے والے سے روایت ہے کہ حضرت عمر بن خطاب نے لشکر کے ایک افسر کو لکھا؛ کہ مجھے یہ خبر پہنچی ہے کہ بعض لوگ تم میں سے کافر عجمی کو بلاتے ہیں جب وہ پہاڑ پر چڑھ جاتا ہے اور لڑائی سے باز آتا ہے، تو ایک شخص اس سے کہتا ہے مت ڈر، پھر قابو پاکر اس کو مار ڈالاتا ہے؛ قسم اس ذات کی جس کے قبضے میں میری جان ہے اگر میں کسی کو ایسا کرتے جان لوں گا تو اس کی گردن ماروں گا۔</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Kufa ke rehne wale se riwayat hai ke Hazrat Umar bin Khattab (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne lashkar ke ek afsar ko likha: Mujhe ye khabar pahunchi hai ke baaz log tum mein se kafir ajami ko bulate hain jab woh pahad par chadh jata hai aur ladai se baaz aata hai, to ek shakhs usse kehta hai mat dar, phir qabu pakar usko maar dalta hai. Qasam us zaat ki jiske qabze mein meri jaan hai, agar main kisi ko aisa karte jaan lunga to uski gardan maar dunga.</p>

<h3>Arabic</h3>

<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّهُ كَانَ إِذَا أَعْطَى شَيْئًا فِي سَبِيلِ اللَّهِ يَقُولُ لِصَاحِبِهِ إِذَا بَلَغْتَ وَادِيَ الْقُرَى فَشَأْنَكَ بِهِ ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Umar (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi that when Abdullah ibn Umar gave something in the way of Allah, he would say to its owner, &quot;When you reach Wadi&#x27;l-Qura, (on the outskirts of Madina) then it is your affair</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 971]</p>

## Narration 966

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A972)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 21</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 972]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/972" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 972</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   972 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، أَنَّ سَعِيدَ بْنَ الْمُسَيَّبِ، كَانَ يَقُولُ إِذَا أُعْطِيَ الرَّجُلُ الشَّىْءَ فِي الْغَزْوِ فَيَبْلُغُ بِهِ رَأْسَ مَغْزَاتِهِ فَهُوَ لَهُ ‏.‏ وَسُئِلَ مَالِكٌ عَنْ رَجُلٍ أَوْجَبَ عَلَى نَفْسِهِ الْغَزْوَ فَتَجَهَّزَ حَتَّى إِذَا أَرَادَ أَنْ يَخْرُجَ مَنَعَهُ أَبَوَاهُ أَوْ أَحَدُهُمَا فَقَالَ لاَ يُكَابِرْهُمَا وَلَكِنْ يُؤَخِّرُ ذَلِكَ إِلَى عَامٍ آخَرَ فَأَمَّا الْجِهَازُ فَإِنِّي أَرَى أَنْ يَرْفَعَهُ حَتَّى يَخْرُجَ بِهِ فَإِنْ خَشِيَ أَنْ يَفْسُدَ بَاعَهُ وَأَمْسَكَ ثَمَنَهُ حَتَّى يَشْتَرِيَ بِهِ مَا يُصْلِحُهُ لِلْغَزْوِ فَإِنْ كَانَ مُوسِرًا يَجِدُ مِثْلَ جِهَازِهِ إِذَا خَرَجَ فَلْيَصْنَعْ بِجِهَازِهِ مَا شَاءَ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/972" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Said ibn al-Musayyab (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Yahya ibn Said that Said ibn al-Musayyab used to say, &quot;When a man is given something to use in a military expedition, and he brings it to the battlefield, it is his.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 972]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/972" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 972</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   972 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik was asked about a man who pledged himself to go on a military campaign, equipped himself,and when he wanted to go out, one or both of his parents prevented him. He said, &quot;He should not contradict them. Let him put it off for another year. As for the equipment, I think that he should store it until he needs it. If he fears that it will spoil, let him sell it and keep its price so that he can readily buy what is needed fora military expedition. If he is well-to-do, he will find the like of his equipment when he goes out, so let him do what he likes with his equipment</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/972" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

## Narration 967

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A973)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 21</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم بَعَثَ سَرِيَّةً فِيهَا عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ عُمَرَ قِبَلَ نَجْدٍ فَغَنِمُوا إِبِلاً كَثِيرَةً فَكَانَ سُهْمَانُهُمُ اثْنَىْ عَشَرَ بَعِيرًا أَوْ أَحَدَ عَشَرَ بَعِيرًا وَنُفِّلُوا بَعِيرًا بَعِيرًا ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 973]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/973" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi from Abdullah ibn Umar that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, sent a raiding party which included Abdullah ibn Umar near Najd. They plundered many camels and their portions were twelve or eleven camels each. They divided it up camel by camel</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 973]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/973" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने एक लश्कर नजद की तरफ़ रवाना किया जिसमें अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर भी थे, तो गनीमत में बहुत से ऊंट हासिल हुए और हर एक का हिस्सा बारह या ग्यारह ऊंट था, और एक ऊंट ज़्यादा दिया गया।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 973]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/973" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن عمر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے ایک لشکر نجد کی طرف روانہ کیا جس میں عبداللہ بن عمر تھے، تو غنیمت میں بہت سے اونٹ حاصل کئے اور ہر ایک کا حصہ بارہ اونٹ یا گیارہ اونٹ تھے، اور ایک اونٹ زیادہ دیا گیا ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 973]</p>

## Narration 968

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A974)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 21</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 974]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/974" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 974</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   974 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، أَنَّهُ سَمِعَ سَعِيدَ بْنَ الْمُسَيَّبِ، يَقُولُ كَانَ النَّاسُ فِي الْغَزْوِ إِذَا اقْتَسَمُوا غَنَائِمَهُمْ يَعْدِلُونَ الْبَعِيرَ بِعَشْرِ شِيَاهٍ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فِي الأَجِيرِ فِي الْغَزْوِ إِنَّهُ إِنْ كَانَ شَهِدَ الْقِتَالَ وَكَانَ مَعَ النَّاسِ عِنْدَ الْقِتَالِ وَكَانَ حُرًّا فَلَهُ سَهْمُهُ وَإِنْ لَمْ يَفْعَلْ ذَلِكَ فَلاَ سَهْمَ لَهُ وَأَرَى أَنْ لاَ يُقْسَمَ إِلاَّ لِمَنْ شَهِدَ الْقِتَالَ مِنَ الأَحْرَارِ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/974" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Said ibn al-Musayyab (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Yahya ibn Said that he heard Said ibn al-Musayyab say, &quot;When people in military expeditions divided the spoils, they made a camel equal to ten sheep.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 974]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/974" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 974</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   974 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said about the paid labourer in military expeditions, &quot;If he is present at the battle and is with the people in the battle and he is a free man, he has his share. If he is not present, he has no share.&quot; Malik summed up, &quot;I think that the booty is only divided among free men who have been present at the battle</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/974" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 969

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A975)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 21</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 975]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/975" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 975</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   975 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ عَبْدًا، لِعَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ أَبَقَ وَأَنَّ فَرَسًا لَهُ عَارَ فَأَصَابَهُمَا الْمُشْرِكُونَ ثُمَّ غَنِمَهُمَا الْمُسْلِمُونَ فَرُدَّا عَلَى عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ وَذَلِكَ قَبْلَ أَنْ تُصِيبَهُمَا الْمَقَاسِمُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/975" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Umar (companion). The distinct source report is selected from within the long legal discussion. All surrounding compiler explanations remain complete source context.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that it reached him that a slave of Abdullah ibn Umar escaped and one of his horses wandered off, and the idol worshippers seized them. Then the Muslims recaptured them, and they were returned to Abdullah ibn Umar, before the division of the spoils took place.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 975]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/975" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 975</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   975 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Malik said about enemy soldiers who were found on the seashore of a Muslim land, and they claimed that they were merchants and that the sea had driven them ashore, while the Muslims were not able to verify any of that except that their ships were damaged, or they were thirsty and had disembarked without the permission of the Muslims, &quot;I think that it is up to the imam to give his opinion about them, and I do not think that the tax of one fifth is taken from them.&quot; Malik said, &quot;I do not see that there is any harm in the Muslims eating whatever food they come across in enemy territory before the spoils are divided.&quot; Malik said, &quot;I think that any camels, cattle and sheep (taken as booty) are considered as food which the Muslims can eat in enemy territory. If they could not be eaten until the people had gathered for the division and the spoils had been distributed among them, that would be harmful for the army. I do not see any objection to eating such things within acceptable limits. I do not think, however, that anyone should store up any of it to take back to his family.&quot; Malik was asked whether it was proper for a man who obtained food in enemy territory and ate some of it and made provision so that there was some of it left over to keep and eat with his family, or to sell before he had come to his country and make use of its price. He said, &quot;If he sells it while he is on a military expedition, I think that he should put its price into the booty of the Muslims. If he takes it back to his country, I see no objection to his eating it and using it if it is a small insignificant thing.&quot; </p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/975" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="ltr"> I heard Malik say about muslim property that had been seized by the enemy, &quot;If it is noticed before the distribution, then it is returned to itsowner. Whatever has already been distributed is not returned to anyone.&quot; Malik, when asked about a man whose young male slave was taken by the idol worshippers and then the Muslims re-captured him, said, &quot;The owner is more entitled to him without having to pay his price or value or having to incur any loss before the distribution takes place. If the distribution has already taken place then I think that the slave belongs to his master for his price if the master wants him back.&quot; Regarding an umm walad of a Muslim man who has been taken by the idol worshippers and then recaptured by the Muslims and allotted in the distribution of spoils and then recognised by her master after the distribution, Malik said, &quot;She is not to be enslaved. I think that the Imam should pay a ransom for her for her master. If he does not do it, then her master must pay a ransom for her and not leave her. I do not think that she should be made a slave by whoever takes her and intercourse with her is not halal. She is in the position of a free woman because her master would be required to pay compensation if she injured somebody and so she is in the same position (as a wife). He must not leave the mother of his son to be enslaved nor may intercourse with her be made halal.&quot; Malik was asked about a man who went to enemy territory to pay ransom or to trade, and he bought a free man or a slave, or they were given to him. He said, &quot;As for the free man, the price he buys him for is a debt against the man and he is not made a slave. If the captive is given to him freely, he is free and owes nothing unless the man gave something in recompense for him. That is a debt against the free man, the same as if a ransom had been paid for him. As for a slave, his former master can choose to take him back and pay his price to the man who bought him or he can choose to leave him, as he wishes. If he was given to the man, the former master is more entitled to him, and he owes nothing for him unless the man gave something for him in recompense. Whatever he gave for him is a loss against the master if he wants him back</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/975" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 975</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   975 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">इमाम मालिक ने दुश्मन के उन फ़ौजियों के बारे में कहा जो मुसलमानों की सरज़मीन के साहिल पर पाए गए और उन्होंने दावा किया के वो ताज़िर हैं और समंदर उन्हें यहाँ ले आया है, जबकि मुसलमानों के पास इस की तस्दीक का कोई ज़रिया न हो सिवाए इस के के उन के जहाज़ टूट चुके हों, या वो प्यासे हों और उन्होंने मुसलमानों की इजाज़त के बग़ैर क़दम रखा हो। इमाम मालिक ने फ़रमाया, &#x27;मेरा ख़याल है के उन के बारे में फ़ैसला करना इमाम का काम है, और मुझे नहीं लगता के उन से ख़ुम्स (पांचवां हिस्सा) लिया जाए गा।&#x27; इमाम मालिक ने मज़ीद कहा, &#x27;मैं इस में कोई हर्ज नहीं समझता के मुसलमान दुश्मन के इलाक़े में मिलने वाली ख़ुराक खाएं इस से पहले के माल-ए-गनीमत तक़सीम किया जाए।&#x27;</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/975" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 975]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/975" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Urdu · references (1)</summary>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   975 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">امام مالک نے دشمن کے ان فوجیوں کے بارے میں کہا جو مسلمانوں کی سرزمین کے ساحل پر پائے گئے اور انہوں نے دعویٰ کیا کہ وہ تاجر ہیں اور سمندر انہیں یہاں لے آیا ہے، جبکہ مسلمانوں کے پاس اس کی تصدیق کا کوئی ذریعہ نہ ہو سوائے اس کے کہ ان کے جہاز ٹوٹ چکے ہوں، یا وہ پیاسے ہوں اور انہوں نے مسلمانوں کی اجازت کے بغیر قدم رکھا ہو۔ امام مالک نے فرمایا، &#x27;میرا خیال ہے کہ ان کے بارے میں فیصلہ کرنا امام کا کام ہے، اور مجھے نہیں لگتا کہ ان سے خمس (پانچواں حصہ) لیا جائے گا۔&#x27; امام مالک نے مزید کہا، &#x27;میں اس میں کوئی حرج نہیں سمجھتا کہ مسلمان دشمن کے علاقے میں ملنے والی خوراک کھائیں اس سے پہلے کہ مال غنیمت تقسیم کیا جائے۔&#x27; (مختصر)</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/975" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Imam Malik ne dushman ke un faujiyon ke bare mein kaha jo musalmanon ki sarzameen ke sahil par paye gaye aur unhon ne dawa kiya ke woh tajir hain aur samundar unhein yahan le aaya hai, jabke musalmanon ke paas is ki tasdeeq ka koi zariya na ho siwaye is ke ke un ke jahaz toot chuke hon, ya woh pyase hon aur unhon ne musalmanon ki ijazat ke baghair qadam rakha ho. Imam Malik ne farmaya, &#x27;Mera khayal hai ke un ke bare mein faisla karna Imam ka kaam hai, aur mujhe nahi lagta ke un se khums (panchwan hissa) liya jaye ga.&#x27; Imam Malik ne mazeed kaha, &#x27;Main is mein koi harj nahi samajhta ke musalman dushman ke ilaqe mein milne wali khorak khayen is se pehle ke maal-e-ghanimat taqseem kiya jaye.&#x27;</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/975" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>
